.width=Math.max(1,Math.round(w));
    this.canvas.height=Math.max(1,Math.round(h));
    if(old&&old.width===this.canvas.width&&old.height===this.canvas.height)this.present(old);
    else this.clear();
    this.setZoom(this.zoom);
  }
  present(frame){
    if(!frame||!Number.isFinite(frame.width)||!Number.isFinite(frame.height)||!frame.rgba)throw new RenderExecutionError('Invalid framebuffer payload');
    const w=Math.max(1,Math.round(frame.width)),h=Math.max(1,Math.round(frame.height));
    const expected=w*h*4;
    if(frame.rgba.length!==expected)throw new RenderExecutionError(`Framebuffer byte length mismatch: ${frame.rgba.length} != ${expected}`);
    if(this.canvas.width!==w||this.canvas.height!==h){this.canvas.width=w;this.canvas.height=h;}
    const pixels=frame.rgba instanceof Uint8ClampedArray?frame.rgba:new Uint8ClampedArray(frame.rgba);
    this.ctx.putImageData(new ImageData(pixels,w,h),0,0);
    this.lastFrame={width:w,height:h,rgba:new Uint8ClampedArray(pixels),metadata:frame.metadata||null};
    this.hasValidFrame=true;
    this.lastUpdated=performance.now();
    this.setZoom(this.zoom);
    return true;
  }
  snapshot(){
    if(!this.hasValidFrame||!this.lastFrame)return null;
    return {width:this.lastFrame.width,height:this.lastFrame.height,rgba:new Uint8ClampedArray(this.lastFrame.rgba),metadata:this.lastFrame.metadata};
  }
  setZoom(value){
    this.zoom=Math.max(.1,Math.min(8,Number(value)||1));
    this.canvas.style.width=(this.canvas.width*this.zoom)+'px';
    this.canvas.style.height=(this.canvas.height*this.zoom)+'px';
  }
  fit(){
    const wrap=document.getElementById('renderCanvasWrap');if(!wrap)return;
    const zx=(wrap.clientWidth-12)/Math.max(1,this.canvas.width);
    const zy=(wrap.clientHeight-12)/Math.max(1,this.canvas.height);
    this.setZoom(Math.max(.1,Math.min(zx,zy)));
  }
  oneToOne(){this.setZoom(1);}
}

class RenderUIShell{
  constructor(){
    this.menu=null;this.settingsWindow=null;this.frameWindow=null;this.frameBuffer=null;
  }
  cache(){
    this.menu=document.getElementById('renderMenuPopup');
    this.settingsWindow=document.getElementById('renderSettingsWindow');
    this.frameWindow=document.getElementById('renderFrameWindow');
    this.frameBuffer=new RenderFrameBufferView(document.getElementById('renderCanvas'));
  }
  open(el){el?.classList.add('open');el?.setAttribute('aria-hidden','false');}
  close(el){el?.classList.remove('open');el?.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');}
  updateProgress(p){
    const text=(id,v)=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.textContent=v;};
    text('rfStatus',p.status);
    const bar=document.getElementById('renderProgress');if(bar)bar.value=p.percent||0;
    text('rfSamples',`Samples: ${p.currentSamples||0} / ${p.maximumSamples||0}`);
    text('rfNoise',`Noise: ${p.noise==null?'—':Number(p.noise).toFixed(4)}`);
    text('rfThreads',`Threads: ${p.threads||'—'}`);
    text('rfResolution',`${p.resolution?.[0]||0}×${p.resolution?.[1]||0}`);
    const timing=p.elapsedMs>0?` • ${(p.elapsedMs/1000).toFixed(2)}s`:'';
    text('rfGI',`GI: ${p.gi||'—'}${timing}`);
  }
}

function setRenderGroup1FutureControlsState(){
  const deferred=['rsPrimaryGI'];
  for(const id of deferred){
    const el=document.getElementById(id);
    if(el){el.disabled=true;el.title='Deferred to a later renderer group';}
  }
  for(const id of ['rsGI','rsSecondaryGI','rsBounces','rsProgressive','rsAdaptive','rsMinSamples','rsMaxSamples','rsNoise','rsDenoise','rsToneMap','rsExposure','rsLightTree','rsPathGuiding']){
    const el=document.getElementById(id);
    if(el){el.disabled=false;el.title='';}
  }
  const denoise=document.getElementById('rsDenoise');if(denoise&&!['Off','Fast','Quality'].includes(denoise.value))denoise.value='Off';

  const format=document.getElementById('rsFormat');
  if(format){
    [...format.options].forEach(o=>{if(o.value==='EXR'||o.text==='EXR')o.disabled=true;});
    if(format.value==='EXR')format.value='PNG';
  }
  const depth=document.getElementById('rsDepth');
  if(depth){
    [...depth.options].forEach(o=>o.disabled=o.text!=='8-bit');
    depth.value='8-bit';
    depth.title='16/32-bit output is deferred until the render channel/export architecture is implemented';
  }
}


const RenderGroup1Contract=Object.freeze({
  owns:Object.freeze([
    'render menu',
    'render settings UI shell',
    'render framebuffer UI shell',
    'render request settings',
    'render job lifecycle state'
  ]),
  doesNotOwn:Object.freeze([
    'editor scene objects',
    'MNMesh topology',
    'selection',
    'transforms',
    'viewport materials',
    'editor undo history',
    'render scene extraction',
    'ray tracing',
    'BVH',
    'lighting',
    'path integration'
  ]),
  extensionPoints:Object.freeze([
    'RenderFramework.createJob',
    'RenderJobController.start',
    'RenderFrameBufferView',
    'RenderFramework.render'
  ])
});
window.RenderGroup1Contract=RenderGroup1Contract;

function validateRenderGroup1(){
  const errors=[];
  const ids=[
    'renderMenuBtn','renderMenuPopup','renderSettingsWindow','renderFrameWindow',
    'renderNowBtn','renderSettingsBtn','renderLastBtn','renderRegionBtn',
    'renderStopBtn','showRenderWindowBtn','renderCanvas','renderProgress'
  ];
  for(const id of ids)if(!document.getElementById(id))errors.push('Missing '+id);
  if(!(RenderFramework.settings instanceof RenderSettingsModel))errors.push('Render settings model unavailable');
  if(!(RenderFramework.ui instanceof RenderUIShell))errors.push('Render UI shell unavailable');
  if(typeof RenderFramework.render!=='function')errors.push('Render API unavailable');
  if(!RenderGroup1Contract?.extensionPoints?.length)errors.push('Render Group 1 contract unavailable');
  if(RenderFramework.initialized && !RenderFramework.jobs)errors.push('Render job controller unavailable');
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

function initRenderGroup1(){
  if(RenderFramework.initialized)return true;

  RenderFramework.ui.cache();
  setRenderGroup1FutureControlsState();
  bindLightUI();
  RenderFramework.jobs=new RenderJobController(p=>RenderFramework.ui.updateProgress(p));
  const configuredThreads=RenderFramework.settings.cpuThreads;
  if(configuredThreads!=='Auto'){
    RenderFramework.jobs.workerPool.size=Math.max(1,Math.min(Number(configuredThreads)||1,16));
  }

  const menuBtn=document.getElementById('renderMenuBtn');
  const menu=RenderFramework.ui.menu;

  menuBtn?.addEventListener('click',e=>{
    e.stopPropagation();
    const r=menuBtn.getBoundingClientRect();
    menu.style.left=r.left+'px';
    menu.style.top=r.bottom+'px';
    const open=menu.classList.toggle('open');
    menu.setAttribute('aria-hidden',open?'false':'true');
  });

  document.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
    if(menu?.classList.contains('open')&&!menu.contains(e.target)&&e.target!==menuBtn){
      menu.classList.remove('open');
      menu.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
    }
  });

  function finish(job){
    if(!state.active||state.active.renderId!==(job?.id??null))return;
    state.last={schema:1,version:VERSION,code:null,name:'Render completed',severity:'info',category:'rendering',detectedAt:now(),renderId:job?.id??null,stage:'complete',message:'Render completed successfully',status:'resolved',evidence:jobEvidence(job)};
    state.active=null;renderPanel();
  }
  function fail(job){
    const e=job?.error;const code=classify(e,e?.stage||'render');
    record(code,{renderId:job?.id,stage:e?.stage||'render',message:String(e?.message||e||'Render failed'),evidence:jobEvidence(job),lastSuccessfulStage:state.active?.lastStatus||''});
    state.active=null;
  }

  function tick(){
    const job=getActive();
    if(job&&!state.active)begin(job);
    if(!job){renderPanel();return;}
    if(state.active&&state.active.renderId!==(job.id??null))begin(job);
    const a=state.active;if(!a)return;
    const status=String(job.status||job.progress?.status||'');
    const pct=num(job.progress?.percent)||0;
    if(pct>a.lastPercent+0.0001||status!==a.lastStatus){a.lastPercent=pct;a.lastStatus=status;a.lastProgressAt=perf();}
    const age=perf()-a.lastProgressAt;
    if(/rendering/i.test(status)&&age>=STALL_MS&&!a.stallReported){
      a.stallReported=true;record('RND-0101',{renderId:job.id,stage:'render',message:'Render made no measurable progress for '+Math.round(age)+' ms',evidence:jobEvidence(job),recommendedAction:'Inspect current pass, ray counts, recursion and material/light state.'});
    }
    if(job.error||/error/i.test(status)){fail(job);return;}
    if(job.finished||/finished|complete/i.test(status)){finish(job);return;}
    renderPanel();
  }

  function snapshot(){return Object.freeze({version:VERSION,codes:CODES,active:safe(state.active),last:safe(state.last),lastCode:state.lastCode,reports:Object.freeze(state.reports.map(r=>Object.freeze({...r})))});}
  function clear(){state.reports.length=0;state.last=null;state.lastCode=null;renderPanel();return true;}
  function exportReport(){return JSON.stringify(snapshot(),null,2);}

  function renderPanel(){

      'Render ID: '+String(ev.renderId??'—'),'Stage: '+String(ev.errorStage||state.last?.stage||ev.status||'—'),
      'Resolution: '+(Array.isArray(ev.resolution)?ev.resolution.join('×'):'—'),'Samples: '+String(ev.currentSamples??'—')+'/'+String(ev.maximumSamples??'—'),

      'Shadow rays: '+String(ev.shadowRays??'—'),'Reflection rays: '+String(ev.reflectionRays??'—'),'Refraction rays: '+String(ev.refractionRays??'—'),
      'Intersections: '+String(ev.intersections??'—'),'Misses: '+String(ev.misses??'—'),'Invalid rays: '+String(ev.invalidRays??'—'),
      state.last?.message?('Last: '+state.last.message):''
    ].filter(Boolean).join('\n');
  }

  function fail(job){
    const e=job?.error;const code=classify(e,e?.stage||'render');
    record(code,{renderId:job?.id,stage:e?.stage||'render',message:String(e?.message||e||'Render failed'),evidence:jobEvidence(job),lastSuccessfulStage:state.active?.lastStatus||''});
    state.active=null;
  }

  function tick(){
    const job=getActive();
    if(job&&!state.active)begin(job);
    if(!job){renderPanel();return;}
    if(state.active&&state.active.renderId!==(job.id??null))begin(job);
    const a=state.active;if(!a)return;
    const status=String(job.status||job.progress?.status||'');
    const pct=num(job.progress?.percent)||0;
    if(pct>a.lastPercent+0.0001||status!==a.lastStatus){a.lastPercent=pct;a.lastStatus=status;a.lastProgressAt=perf();}
    const age=perf()-a.lastProgressAt;
    if(/rendering/i.test(status)&&age>=STALL_MS&&!a.stallReported){
      a.stallReported=true;record('RND-0101',{renderId:job.id,stage:'render',message:'Render made no measurable progress for '+Math.round(age)+' ms',evidence:jobEvidence(job),recommendedAction:'Inspect current pass, ray counts, recursion and material/light state.'});
    }
    if(job.error||/error/i.test(status)){fail(job);return;}
    if(job.finished||/finished|complete/i.test(status)){finish(job);return;}
    renderPanel();
  }

  function snapshot(){return Object.freeze({version:VERSION,codes:CODES,active:safe(state.active),last:safe(state.last),lastCode:state.lastCode,reports:Object.freeze(state.reports.map(r=>Object.freeze({...r})))});}
  function clear(){state.reports.length=0;state.last=null;state.lastCode=null;renderPanel();return true;}
  function exportReport(){return JSON.stringify(snapshot(),null,2);}

  function renderPanel(){

  }

function get3DLiteBootstrapSnapshot(){
  return Object.freeze({
    lifecycle:ThreeDLiteAppContext.lifecycle,
    phase:ThreeDLiteAppContext.phase,
    services:Object.freeze(Object.keys(ThreeDLiteAppContext.services)),
    failures:Object.freeze(ThreeDLiteAppContext.failures.slice()),
    dependencyCheck:validate3DLiteServiceDependencies()
  });
}
window.get3DLiteBootstrapSnapshot=get3DLiteBootstrapSnapshot;

function validate3DLiteServiceDependencies(){
  const missing=[];
  for(const [name,deps] of Object.entries(ThreeDLiteSubsystemRegistry.dependencies)){
    if(!ThreeDLiteAppContext.get(name))continue;
    for(const dep of deps)if(!ThreeDLiteAppContext.get(dep))missing.push(`${name}->${dep}`);
  }
  return Object.freeze({ok:missing.length===0,missing});
}
window.validate3DLiteServiceDependencies=validate3DLiteServiceDependencies;

function freezeRenderSnapshot(v){
  if(!v||typeof v!=='object'||Object.isFrozen(v)||ArrayBuffer.isView(v))return v;
  Object.freeze(v);for(const k of Object.keys(v))freezeRenderSnapshot(v[k]);return v;
}
class RenderMeshSnapshot{
  constructor(d){
    this.positions=d.positions;this.normals=d.normals;this.uvs=d.uvs;
    this.indices=d.indices;this.materialIndices=d.materialIndices;
    this.bounds=freezeRenderSnapshot(d.bounds);Object.freeze(this);
  }
}

  }

  if(!v||typeof v!=='object'||Object.isFrozen(v)||ArrayBuffer.isView(v))return v;
  Object.freeze(v);for(const k of Object.keys(v))freezeRenderSnapshot(v[k]);return v;
}

class RenderGeometryPacket{
  constructor(data){
    this.objectId=data.objectId;
    this.name=data.name;
    this.positions=data.positions;
    this.normals=data.normals;
    this.uvs=data.uvs;
    this.indices=data.indices;
    this.materialIndices=data.materialIndices;
    this.bounds=freezeRenderSnapshot(data.bounds);
    Object.freeze(this);
  }
}

const RenderGeometryValidation=Object.freeze({
  validatePacket(packet){
    const errors=[];
    if(!packet)errors.push('Missing geometry packet');
    const p=packet?.positions,i=packet?.indices,n=packet?.normals,u=packet?.uvs,m=packet?.materialIndices;
    if(!ArrayBuffer.isView(p)||p.length%3)errors.push('Malformed position buffer');
    if(!ArrayBuffer.isView(i)||i.length%3)errors.push('Malformed index buffer');
    const vc=(p?.length||0)/3,tc=(i?.length||0)/3;
    if(n?.length&&n.length!==p.length)errors.push('Normal count mismatch');
    if(u?.length&&u.length!==vc*2)errors.push('UV count mismatch');
    if(m?.length!==tc)errors.push('Material-index count mismatch');
    if(p)for(let k=0;k<p.length;k++)if(!Number.isFinite(p[k])){errors.push('Non-finite position');break}
    if(n)for(let k=0;k<n.length;k++)if(!Number.isFinite(n[k])){errors.push('Non-finite normal');break}
    if(u)for(let k=0;k<u.length;k++)if(!Number.isFinite(u[k])){errors.push('Non-finite UV');break}
    if(i)for(let k=0;k<i.length;k++)if(!Number.isInteger(i[k])||i[k]<0||i[k]>=vc){errors.push('Index out of range');break}
    return {ok:errors.length===0,errors,vertexCount:vc,triangleCount:tc};
  }
});

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

const RenderDenoiser13=Object.freeze({
  process(channels,mode='Off'){
    const beauty=channels?.get?.('Beauty');
    if(!beauty)return null;
    const w=channels.width|0,h=channels.height|0;
    if(w<1||h<1)return null;
    if(mode==='Off')return new Float32Array(beauty);

    const albedo=channels.get('Albedo');
    const normal=channels.get('Normal');
    const depth=channels.get('Depth');
    const radius=mode==='Quality'?2:1;
    const out=new Float32Array(beauty.length);

    const colorDist=(a,b)=>{
      const dr=beauty[a*3]-beauty[b*3],dg=beauty[a*3+1]-beauty[b*3+1],db=beauty[a*3+2]-beauty[b*3+2];
      return dr*dr+dg*dg+db*db;
    };
    const guideWeight=(a,b)=>{
      let wgt=1;
      if(albedo){
        const dr=albedo[a*3]-albedo[b*3],dg=albedo[a*3+1]-albedo[b*3+1],db=albedo[a*3+2]-albedo[b*3+2];
        wgt*=Math.exp(-(dr*dr+dg*dg+db*db)*8);
      }
      if(normal){
        const d=normal[a*3]*normal[b*3]+normal[a*3+1]*normal[b*3+1]+normal[a*3+2]*normal[b*3+2];
        wgt*=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(d-.5)*2));
      }
      if(depth){
        const da=depth[a],db=depth[b],scale=Math.max(1e-4,Math.max(Math.abs(da),Math.abs(db)));
        wgt*=Math.exp(-Math.abs(da-db)/(scale*.03+1e-4));
      }
      return wgt;
    };

    for(let y=0;y<h;y++){
      for(let x=0;x<w;x++){
        const i=y*w+x;
        let r=0,g=0,b=0,sum=0;
        for(let oy=-radius;oy<=radius;oy++){
          const yy=Math.max(0,Math.min(h-1,y+oy));
          for(let ox=-radius;ox<=radius;ox++){
            const xx=Math.max(0,Math.min(w-1,x+ox)),j=yy*w+xx;
            const spatial=Math.exp(-(ox*ox+oy*oy)/(radius===2?3:1.5));
            const edge=Math.exp(-colorDist(i,j)*2.5);
            const wt=Math.max(1e-8,spatial*edge*guideWeight(i,j));
            r+=beauty[j*3]*wt;g+=beauty[j*3+1]*wt;b+=beauty[j*3+2]*wt;sum+=wt;
          }
        }
        const inv=1/Math.max(1e-8,sum);
        out[i*3]=r*inv;out[i*3+1]=g*inv;out[i*3+2]=b*inv;
      }
    }
    return out;
  }
});

  get(name){return window.ThreeDLiteAppContext?.get?.(name)||null

s.map(w=>({
        id:w.__renderWorkerId,
        state:w.__renderState||'unknown',
        jobId:w.__renderJobId??null,
        taskId:w.__renderTaskId??null,
        tasks:w.__perf?.tasks||0,
        totalMs:Math.round(w.__perf?.totalMs||0),
        lastMs:Math.round(w.__perf?.lastMs||0),
        failures:w.__perf?.failures||0,
        busyMs:w.__renderBusy?Math.round(performance.now()-(w.__perf?.busySince||performance.now())):0,

  }

  dispose(){
    if(this.disposed)return;
    this.disposed=true;
    this.generation++;

    for(const task of this.queue){
      task.reject(new WorkerError('Worker pool disposed'));
    }
    this.queue=[];

    for(const task of this.activeTasks.values()){
      task.reject(new WorkerError('Worker pool disposed'));
    }
    this.activeTasks.clear();

    for(const worker of [...this.workers]){
      try{worker.terminate();}catch(_){}
    }
    this.workers=[];this.idle=[];

    if(this.workerURL){
      URL.revokeObjectURL(this.workerURL);
      this.workerURL=null;
    }
  }
}

class PreparedGeometrySet{
  constructor(items){
    this.items=Object.freeze(items.slice());
    this.objectCount=items.length;
    this.vertexCount=items.reduce((n,x)=>n+x.vertexCount,0);
    this.triangleCount=items.reduce((n,x)=>n+x.triangleCount,0);
    Object.freeze(this);
  }
}

var RenderGeometryPreparation={
  mainThread(job,packet){
    if(job.cancelled)throw Object.assign(new Error('Render job cancelled'),{cancelled:true});

    const vertexCount=packet.positions.length/3;
    const triangleCount=packet.indices.length/3;

    for(let i=0;i<packet.indices.length;i++){
      if(packet.indices[i]>=vertexCount)throw new GeometryCompileError('Index out of range in main-thread worker fallback');
    }

    return {
      type:'geometryPrepared',
      jobId:job.id,
      objectId:packet.objectId,
      vertexCount,
      triangleCount,
      bounds:new Float32Array([
        ...packet.bounds.min,
        ...packet.bounds.max
      ]),
      triangleIndices:new Uint32Array(packet.indices)
    };
  },

  async prepare(job,pool,onProgress=()=>{}){
    const packets=RenderGeometryCompiler.compileScene(job.renderScene);
    if(!packets.length)return new PreparedGeometrySet([]);

    const items=[];
    const total=packets.length;

    for(let i=0;i<packets.length;i++){
      if(job.cancelled)throw Object.assign(new Error('Render job cancelled'),{cancelled:true});

      while(job.paused&&!job.cancelled){
        await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,25));
      }

      const packet=packets[i];
      let result;

      try{
        result=await pool.run(
          {type:'prepareGeometry',jobId:job.id,packet},
          RenderGeometryCompiler.transferables(packet)
        );
      }catch(err){
        if(err?.workerUnavailable){
          result=this.mainThread(job,packet);
        }else{
          throw err;
        }
      }

      items.push(Object.freeze({
        objectId:result.objectId,
        vertexCount:result.vertexCount,
        triangleCount:result.triangleCount,
        bounds:Object.freeze(Array.from(result.bounds||[])),
        triangleIndices:result.triangleIndices
      }));

      onProgress((i+1)/total,items[items.length-1]);
    }

    return new PreparedGeometrySet(items);
  }
};


/* ============================================================
   3DLite v3.82.8 — LiteTrace Renderer v1.1 RC1 ASI Fix — CORE B
   CLEAN GROUP 3 + GROUP 4 CONSOLIDATION
   ============================================================ */
class RenderExecutionGeometry{
  constructor(renderScene){
    this.accelerationPackets=RenderGeometryCompiler.compileScene(renderScene);
    this.workerPackets=this.accelerationPackets.map(p=>new RenderGeometryPacket({
      objectId:p.objectId,name:p.name,
      positions:new Float32Array(p.positions),
      normals:new Float32Array(p.normals),
      uvs:new Float32Array(p.uvs),
      indices:new Uint32Array(p.indices),
      materialIndices:new Uint16Array(p.materialIndices),
      bounds:{min:[...p.bounds.min],max:[...p.bounds.max]}
    }));
    Object.freeze(this.accelerationPackets);
    Object.freeze(this.workerPackets);
  }
}

var RenderCoreB=Object.freeze({
  version:'3.79.16-core-b-part2',

  createExecutionGeometry(renderScene){
    return new RenderExecutionGeometry(renderScene);
  },

  buildAccelerationFromPackets(packets){
    const store=new RenderTriangleStore();
    packets.forEach((p,i)=>store.addPacket(p,i));
    store.finalize();
    return new RenderAccelerationSet(store,new RenderBVH(store,4));
  },

  async prepareWorkerGeometry(job,pool,executionGeometry,onProgress=()=>{}){
    const items=[],packets=executionGeometry.workerPackets,total=packets.length;
    for(let i=0;i<packets.length;i++){
      if(job.cancelled)throw Object.assign(new Error('Render job cancelled'),{cancelled:true});
      while(job.paused&&!job.cancelled)await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,25));

      const packet=packets[i];
      let result;
      try{
        result=await pool.run(
          {type:'prepareGeometry',jobId:job.id,packet},
          RenderGeometryCompiler.transferables(packet)
        );
      }catch(err){
        if(err?.workerUnavailable){job.progress.workerFallback=true;result=RenderGeometryPreparation.mainThread(job,packet);}
        else throw err;
      }

      items.push(Object.freeze({
        objectId:result.objectId,
        vertexCount:result.vertexCount,
        triangleCount:result.triangleCount,
        bounds:Object.freeze(Array.from(result.bounds||[])),
        triangleIndices:result.triangleIndices
      }));
      onProgress((i+1)/Math.max(1,total),items[items.length-1]);
    }
    return new PreparedGeometrySet(items);
  },

  validate(){
    const errors=[];
    const g3=validateRenderGroup3();if(!g3.ok)errors.push(...g3.errors);
    const g4=validateRenderGroup4();if(!g4.ok)errors.push(...g4.errors);
    if(typeof this.createExecutionGeometry!=='function')errors.push('Core B execution geometry missing');
    if(typeof this.buildAccelerationFromPackets!=='function')errors.push('Core B acceleration builder missing');
    return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
  }
});
window.RenderCoreB=RenderCoreB;

const RenderGroup3Contract=Object.freeze({
  owns:Object.freeze([
    'worker pool',
    'render execution queue',
    'worker-safe geometry packets',
    'render geometry preparation',
    'prepared triangle-index payloads'
  ]),
  consumes:Object.freeze([
    'RenderCoreA render jobs',
    'immutable RenderSceneSnapshot'
  ]),
  doesNotOwn:Object.freeze([
    'editor objects',
    'editor geometry buffers',
    'MNMesh',
    'viewport renderer',
    'selection',
    'transforms',
    'editor undo'
  ])
});

function validateRenderGroup3(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof RenderWorkerPool!=='function')errors.push('RenderWorkerPool missing');
  if(typeof RenderGeometryCompiler.compileScene!=='function')errors.push('RenderGeometryCompiler missing');
  if(typeof RenderGeometryValidation.validatePacket!=='function')errors.push('RenderGeometryValidation missing');
  if(typeof RenderGeometryPreparation.prepare!=='function')errors.push('RenderGeometryPreparation missing');
  if(typeof PreparedGeometrySet!=='function')errors.push('PreparedGeometrySet missing');
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

class RenderExecutionGeometry{
  constructor(renderScene){
    this.accelerationPackets=RenderGeometryCompiler.compileScene(renderScene);
    this.workerPackets=this.accelerationPackets.map(p=>new RenderGeometryPacket({
      objectId:p.objectId,name:p.name,
      positions:new Float32Array(p.positions),
      normals:new Float32Array(p.normals),
      uvs:new Float32Array(p.uvs),
      indices:new Uint32Array(p.indices),
      materialIndices:new Uint16Array(p.materialIndices),
      bounds:{min:[...p.bounds.min],max:[...p.bounds.max]}
    }));
    Object.freeze(this.accelerationPackets);
    Object.freeze(this.workerPackets);
  }
}

var RenderCoreB=Object.freeze({
  version:'3.79.16-core-b-part2',

  createExecutionGeometry(renderScene){
    return new RenderExecutionGeometry(renderScene);
  },

  buildAccelerationFromPackets(packets){
    const store=new RenderTriangleStore();
    packets.forEach((p,i)=>store.addPacket(p,i));
    store.finalize();
    return new RenderAccelerationSet(store,new RenderBVH(store,4));
  },

  async prepareWorkerGeometry(job,pool,executionGeometry,onProgress=()=>{}){
    const items=[],packets=executionGeometry.workerPackets,total=packets.length;
    for(let i=0;i<packets.length;i++){
      if(job.cancelled)throw Object.assign(new Error('Render job cancelled'),{cancelled:true});
      while(job.paused&&!job.cancelled)await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,25));

      const packet=packets[i];
      let result;
      try{
        result=await pool.run(
          {type:'prepareGeometry',jobId:job.id,packet},
          RenderGeometryCompiler.transferables(packet)
        );
      }catch(err){
        if(err?.workerUnavailable){job.progress.workerFallback=true;result=RenderGeometryPreparation.mainThread(job,packet);}
        else throw err;
      }

      items.push(Object.freeze({
        objectId:result.objectId,
        vertexCount:result.vertexCount,
        triangleCount:result.triangleCount,
        bounds:Object.freeze(Array.from(result.bounds||[])),
        triangleIndices:result.triangleIndices
      }));
      onProgress((i+1)/Math.max(1,total),items[items.length-1]);
    }
    return new PreparedGeometrySet(items);
  },

  validate(){
    const errors=[];
    const g3=validateRenderGroup3();if(!g3.ok)errors.push(...g3.errors);
    const g4=validateRenderGroup4();if(!g4.ok)errors.push(...g4.errors);
    if(typeof this.createExecutionGeometry!=='function')errors.push('Core B execution geometry missing');
    if(typeof this.buildAccelerationFromPackets!=='function')errors.push('Core B acceleration builder missing');
    return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
  }
});
window.RenderCoreB=RenderCoreB;

const RayMath=Object.freeze({
  EPSILON:1e-6,
  add(a,b){return [a[0]+b[0],a[1]+b[1],a[2]+b[2]]},
  sub(a,b){return [a[0]-b[0],a[1]-b[1],a[2]-b[2]]},
  mul(a,s){return [a[0]*s,a[1]*s,a[2]*s]},
  dot(a,b){return a[0]*b[0]+a[1]*b[1]+a[2]*b[2]},
  cross(a,b){return [a[1]*b[2]-a[2]*b[1],a[2]*b[0]-a[0]*b[2],a[0]*b[1]-a[1]*b[0]]},
  length(a){return Math.hypot(a[0],a[1],a[2])},
  normalize(a){const l=Math.hypot(a[0],a[1],a[2])||1;return [a[0]/l,a[1]/l,a[2]/l]},
  min3(a,b){return [Math.min(a[0],b[0]),Math.min(a[1],b[1]),Math.min(a[2],b[2])]},
  max3(a,b){return [Math.max(a[0],b[0]),Math.max(a[1],b[1]),Math.max(a[2],b[2])]}
});

const RenderRayType=Object.freeze({CAMERA:'camera',SHADOW:'shadow',DIFFUSE:'diffuse',GLOSSY:'glossy',TRANSMISSION:'transmission'});
class RenderRay{
  constructor(origin,direction,tMin=1e-5,tMax=Infinity,type=RenderRayType.CAMERA,depth=0){
    this.origin=origin;this.direction=RayMath.normalize(direction);
    this.invDirection=[
      Math.abs(this.direction[0])<RayMath.EPSILON?Infinity:1/this.direction[0],
      Math.abs(this.direction[1])<RayMath.EPSILON?Infinity:1/this.direction[1],
      Math.abs(this.direction[2])<RayMath.EPSILON?Infinity:1/this.direction[2]
    ];
    this.tMin=tMin;this.tMax=tMax;this.type=type;this.depth=depth;
  }
  at(t){return RayMath.add(this.origin,RayMath.mul(this.direction,t))}
}
const RenderRayPolicy=Object.freeze({
  epsilonForPoint(p){const scale=Math.max(1,Math.abs(p[0]),Math.abs(p[1]),Math.abs(p[2]));return Math.min(1e-2,Math.max(1e-6,scale*1e-7));}
});
Object.assign(window,{RenderRayType,RenderRayPolicy});

  }
}

      ...(scene?.objects||[]).map(o=>`${o.id}:${o.visible!==false?1:0}:${o.mesh?.positions?.length||0}:${o.mesh?.indices?.length||0}`)

/*
  BLAS/TLAS boundary:
  - object mesh geometry revisions feed BLAS-equivalent cached acceleration data;
  - scene/object transform revisions are intentionally kept outside this cache key
    so a future TLAS can update instances without rebuilding object geometry.
  Current browser renderer still uses the proven binary RenderAccelerationSet.
*/
const RenderAccelerationHierarchy16=Object.freeze({
  mode:'geometry-revision acceleration cache; TLAS/BLAS boundary reserved',
  cache:RenderAccelerationCache16,
  build(scene,builder){
    const cached=RenderAccelerationCache16.get(scene);
    if(cached)return cached;
    return RenderAccelerationCache16.put(scene,builder());
  }
});
window.RenderAccelerationCache16=RenderAccelerationCache16;
window.RenderAccelerationHierarchy16=RenderAccelerationHierarchy16;

const RenderAccelerationBuilder=Object.freeze({
  build(renderScene){
    return RenderAccelerationHierarchy16.build(renderScene,()=>{
      const packets=RenderGeometryCompiler.compileScene(renderScene);
      const store=new RenderTriangleStore();
      packets.forEach((p,i)=>store.addPacket(p,i));
      store.finalize();
      return new RenderAccelerationSet(store,new RenderBVH(store,4));
    });
  }
});

  async render(job,acceleration,onProgress=()=>{}){
    const width=Math.min(640,Math.max(1,job.progress.resolution[0]));
    const height=Math.min(360,Math.max(1,job.progress.resolution[1]));
    const rgba=new Uint8ClampedArray(width*height*4);

    const rowChunk=8;
    for(let y=0;y<height;y++){
      if(job.cancelled||job.controlToken16?.cancelled?.())throw Object.assign(new Error('Render job cancelled'),{cancelled:true});
      while((job.paused||job.controlToken16?.paused?.())&&!job.cancelled)await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,20));
      for(let x=0;x<width;x++){
        const ray=RenderCameraRayGenerator.ray(cameraSnapshot,x,y,width,height);
        perf16.addRay(RenderRayType.CAMERA);
        perf16.addRay(RenderRayType.CAMERA);
        const hit=acceleration.trace(ray);
        const o=(y*width+x)*4;
        if(hit.hit){
          const n=hit.geometricNormal;
          rgba[o]=Math.round((n[0]*.5+.5)*255);
          rgba[o+1]=Math.round((n[1]*.5+.5)*255);
          rgba[o+2]=Math.round((n[2]*.5+.5)*255);
          rgba[o+3]=255;
        }else{
          rgba[o]=24;rgba[o+1]=24;rgba[o+2]=24;rgba[o+3]=255;
        }
      }
      if(y%rowChunk===0)onProgress((y+1)/height);
      if(y%rowChunk===0)await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,0));
    }
    return {width,height,rgba};
  }
});

    let hit=TriangleIntersector.intersect(ray,[-1,-1,0],[1,-1,0],[0,1,0]);
    if(!hit.hit||!approx(hit.t,1)||!hit.frontFace||!hit.orientedGeometricNormal)errors.push('Triangle front-face hit failed');

    ray=new RenderRay([0,0,-1],[0,0,1]);
    hit=TriangleIntersector.intersect(ray,[-1,-1,0],[1,-1,0],[0,1,0]);
    if(!hit.hit||hit.frontFace)errors.push('Triangle back-face classification failed');

    ray=new RenderRay([3,3,1],[0,0,-1]);
    if(TriangleIntersector.intersect(ray,[-1,-1,0],[1,-1,0],[0,1,0]).hit)errors.push('Triangle miss failed');

    if(!AABBIntersector.intersect(new RenderRay([0,0,2],[0,0,-1]),[-1,-1,-1],[1,1,1]))errors.push('AABB hit failed');

    const store=new RenderTriangleStore();
    store.addPacket({objectId:'a',positions:new Float32Array([-1,-1,0,1,-1,0,0,1,0,  -1,-1,-3,1,-1,-3,0,1,-3]),normals:new Float32Array(18),uvs:new Float32Array(12),indices:new Uint32Array([0,1,2,3,4,5]),materialIndices:new Uint16Array([0,0])},0);
    store.finalize();
    const bvh=new RenderBVH(store,1),closest=bvh.intersect(new RenderRay([0,0,2],[0,0,-1]));
    if(!closest.hit||!approx(closest.t,2))errors.push('BVH closest-hit failed');
    if(!bvh.anyHit(new RenderRay([0,0,2],[0,0,-1],1e-5,2.1,RenderRayType.SHADOW)))errors.push('BVH any-hit failed');
    if(bvh.anyHit(new RenderRay([0,0,2],[0,0,-1],1e-5,1.9,RenderRayType.SHADOW)))errors.push('Finite shadow tMax failed');
    const bs=bvh.stats();if(bs.nodeCount<1||bs.leafCount<1||bs.triangleCount!==2)errors.push('BVH statistics failed');

    const legacy=new RenderTriangleStore();
    legacy.addPacket({objectId:'legacy',positions:new Float32Array([0,0,0,1,0,0,0,1,0]),indices:new Uint32Array([0,1,2]),materialIndices:new Uint16Array([0])},0);legacy.finalize();
    if(legacy.normals.length!==9||legacy.uvs.length!==6)errors.push('Legacy minimal packet compatibility failed');

    const deg=new RenderTriangleStore();
    deg.addPacket({objectId:'deg',positions:new Float32Array([0,0,0,0,0,0,0,0,0]),indices:new Uint32Array([0,1,2]),materialIndices:new Uint16Array([0])},0);deg.finalize();
    if(deg.triangleCount()!==0||deg.stats.rejectedDegenerate!==1)errors.push('Degenerate rejection failed');

    const empty=new RenderTriangleStore();empty.finalize();const ebvh=new RenderBVH(empty,4);
    if(ebvh.root!==-1||ebvh.intersect(new RenderRay([0,0,0],[0,0,1])).hit||ebvh.anyHit(new RenderRay([0,0,0],[0,0,1])))errors.push('Empty BVH failed');

    const c={position:[0,0,0],quaternion:[0,0,0,1],fov:60,near:0,far:100};
    const cr=RenderCameraRayGenerator.ray(c,49.5,49.5,100,100);
    if(cr.direction[2]>-.99||cr.type!==RenderRayType.CAMERA)errors.push('Camera center ray failed');
    const extreme=RenderCameraRayGenerator.ray({position:[0,0,0],quaternion:[NaN,0,0,1],fov:1000,near:-1,far:0},0,0,1,1);
    if(!extreme.direction.every(Number.isFinite)||extreme.tMin<=0)errors.push('Camera sanitization failed');

    const small=RenderRayPolicy.epsilonForPoint([0,0,0]),large=RenderRayPolicy.epsilonForPoint([1e6,0,0]);
    if(!(small>0&&large>=small&&large<=1e-2))errors.push('Scale-aware epsilon failed');
  }catch(err){errors.push('Group4 self-test exception: '+String(err?.message||err));}
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

var RenderWorkQueue16 = class RenderWorkQueue16{
  constructor(chunks){
    this.queue=chunks.slice();
    this.completed=0;
    this.claimed=0;
    this.steals=0;
  }
  static fromIndices(indices,batchSize=128){
    const chunks=[];
    const n=indices.length;
    const adaptive=Math.max(16,Math.min(512,Math.round(batchSize*(n>250000?.5:n<10000?2:1))));
    for(let i=0;i<n;i+=adaptive){
      chunks.push(typeof indices.subarray==='function'?indices.subarray(i,Math.min(n,i+adaptive)):indices.slice(i,i+adaptive));
    }
    return new RenderWorkQueue16(chunks);
  }
  splitHeavy(maxChunk=128){
    const next=[];
    for(const chunk of this.queue){
      if(chunk.length>maxChunk){
        const mid=Math.ceil(chunk.length/2);
        next.push(chunk.slice(0,mid),chunk.slice(mid));
      }else next.push(chunk);
    }
    this.queue=next;
    return this;
  }
  claim(){
    const work=this.queue.shift()||null;
    if(work){if(this.claimed>0)this.steals++;this.claimed++;}
    return work;
  }
  async run(workerFn,concurrency=1){
    const runners=[];
    const n=Math.max(1,Math.min(8,Number(concurrency)||1));
    for(let w=0;w<n;w++)runners.push((async()=>{
      for(;;){
        const batch=this.claim();
        if(!batch)break;
        await workerFn(batch,w);
        this.completed++;
      }
    })());
    await Promise.all(runners);
    return this.completed;
  }
}
window.RenderWorkQueue16=RenderWorkQueue16;

var RenderTileScheduler16=Object.freeze({
  makeTiles(width,height,tileSize){
    const size=Math.max(4,Math.min(128,Number(tileSize)||16));
    const tiles=[];
    let id=0;
    for(let y=0;y<height;y+=size){
      for(let x=0;x<width;x+=size){
        tiles.push(Object.freeze({
          id:id++,x,y,
          width:Math.min(size,width-x),
          height:Math.min(size,height-y)
        }));
      }
    }
    return tiles;
  },
  async runLocal(job,tiles,runTile,onProgress=()=>{}){
    const total=Math.max(1,tiles.length);
    for(let i=0;i<tiles.length;i++){
      if(job.cancelled)throw Object.assign(new Error('Render job cancelled'),{cancelled:true});
      while(job.paused&&!job.cancelled)await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,20));
      await runTile(tiles[i],i);
      onProgress((i+1)/total);
      if((i&1)===1)await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,0));
    }
  }
});
window.RenderTileScheduler16=RenderTileScheduler16;

var BasicDirectRenderer=Object.freeze({
  shade(job,acceleration,materials,lights,ray,hit){
    const material=RenderMaterialLibrary.resolveHit(job.renderScene,materials,hit);
    if(material.shading==='unlit'){
      return RenderColor.add(material.baseColor,RenderColor.mul(material.emissive,material.emissiveIntensity));
    }

    const n=SurfaceInterpolator.normal(acceleration,hit);
    const v=RayMath.mul(ray.direction,-1);
    let color=RenderColor.mul(material.emissive,material.emissiveIntensity);

    for(const light of lights){
      const sample=DirectLightSampler.sample(light,hit.position);
      if(sample.kind==='ambient'){
        const diffuse=RenderColor.mul(material.baseColor,(1-material.metalness)/Math.PI);
        color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(RenderColor.mul(diffuse,sample.radiance),Math.PI));
      }else{
        const brdf=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(material,n,v,sample.direction);
        color=RenderColor.add(color,RenderColor.mul(brdf,sample.radiance));
      }
    }
    return color;
  },

var RenderCoreC=Object.freeze({
  version:'3.79.09-core-c',

  compileScene(renderScene,settings){
    const materials=RenderMaterialLibrary.compile(renderScene);
    const lights=BasicRenderLighting.create(renderScene,settings);
    return Object.freeze({materials,lights});
  },

  evaluateDirect(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed=1,options={}){
    return DirectLightingIntegrator.evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed,options);
  },

  validate(){

    const g5=validateRenderGroup5();if(!g5.ok)errors.push(...g5.errors);

    const tiles16=RenderTileScheduler16.makeTiles(width,height,RenderCoreH.tileSize(job));

        for(let x=tile.x;x<tile.x+tile.width;x++){
          const ray=RenderCameraRayGenerator.ray(
            job.renderScene.camera,
            rr.x0+x,rr.y0+y,
            rr.fullW,rr.fullH
          );
          const hit=acceleration.trace(ray);
          const o=(y*width+x)*4;
  
          if(hit.hit){

            RenderAOVUtil.writePrimary(aovs,y*width+x,RenderAOVUtil.primary(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed));

            aovs.set3('Global Illumination',y*width+x,[0,0,0]);

            rgba[o+2]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[2])*255);
            rgba[o+3]=255;
          }else{
            const env=RenderPart4Core.environment(job,compiled,ray.direction)||backgroundFallback;
            RenderAOVUtil.writePrimary(aovs,y*width+x,null);
            aovs.set3('Beauty',y*width+x,env);
            aovs.set3('Global Illumination',y*width+x,env);
            aovs.set1('Noise',y*width+x,0);
            const mapped=RenderColor.linearToSrgb(RenderColor.aces(env));
            rgba[o]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[0])*255);
            rgba[o+1]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[1])*255);
            rgba[o+2]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[2])*255);
            rgba[o+3]=job.settings.transparent?0:255;
          }
        }
      }
      perf16.yields++;
    },onProgress);
    RenderCoreH.markStage(job,'render',renderStart16);
    if(LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance===perf16){LiteTraceExecutionContext.activeJob=null;LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance=null;}
    job.renderChannels=aovs;
    return {
      width,height,rgba,
      channels:aovs,
      metadata:Object.freeze({
        renderer:'Part 4 Fast Direct',
        materialCount:compiled.materials.list.length,
        lightCount:compiled.lights.length,
        toneMapping:'ACES',
        colorSpace:'sRGB output / linear shading',
        diagnostics:RenderPart4Core.diagnostics(job,compiled,acceleration),
        performance:RenderCoreH.performance(job)
      })
    };
  }
});

var RenderCoreC=Object.freeze({
  version:'3.79.09-core-c',

  compileScene(renderScene,settings){
    const materials=RenderMaterialLibrary.compile(renderScene);
    const lights=BasicRenderLighting.create(renderScene,settings);
    return Object.freeze({materials,lights});
  },

  evaluateDirect(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed=1,options={}){
    return DirectLightingIntegrator.evaluate(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,hit,pixelSeed,options);
  },

  validate(){
    const errors=[];
    const g5=validateRenderGroup5();if(!g5.ok)errors.push(...g5.errors);
    const g6=validateRenderGroup6();if(!g6.ok)errors.push(...g6.errors);
    if(typeof this.compileScene!=='function')errors.push('Core C scene compiler missing');
    if(typeof this.evaluateDirect!=='function')errors.push('Core C direct evaluator missing');
    return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
  }
});
window.RenderCoreC=RenderCoreC;

const RenderGroup5Contract=Object.freeze({
  owns:Object.freeze([
    'render material compilation',
    'linear color conversion',
    'GGX physical BRDF',
    'basic direct directional lighting',
    'basic environment fill',
    'tone mapping and display conversion',
    'barycentric shading-normal interpolation'
  ]),
  consumes:Object.freeze([
    'Core A material snapshots',
    'Core B ray/BVH hit data',
    'Core A render settings'
  ]),
  doesNotOwn:Object.freeze([
    'editor viewport materials',
    'full scene light object system',
    'shadow visibility',
    'area-light sampling',
    'path GI',
    'light cache',
    'denoising'
  ])
});

function runRenderGroup5SelfTests(){
  const errors=[];
  const approx=(a,b,e=1e-4)=>Math.abs(a-b)<=e;

  const white=RenderColor.srgbToLinear([1,1,1]);
  if(!approx(white[0],1)||!approx(white[1],1)||!approx(white[2],1))errors.push('sRGB white conversion failed');

  const mat=new RenderSurfaceMaterial({
    id:'t',name:'t',shading:'physical',baseColor:[.8,.3,.2],
    roughness:.5,metalness:0,opacity:1,emissive:[0,0,0],emissiveIntensity:0
  });
  const brdf=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(mat,[0,0,1],[0,0,1],[0,0,1]);
  if(brdf.some(x=>!Number.isFinite(x)||x<0))errors.push('Physical BRDF finite/positive test failed');

  const metallic=new RenderSurfaceMaterial({
    id:'m',name:'m',shading:'physical',baseColor:[.9,.7,.2],
    roughness:.25,metalness:1,opacity:1,emissive:[0,0,0],emissiveIntensity:0
  });
  const mb=PhysicalBSDF.evaluate(metallic,[0,0,1],[0,0,1],[0,0,1]);
  if(mb.some(x=>!Number.isFinite(x)||x<0))errors.push('Metallic BRDF test failed');

  const tm=RenderColor.aces([10,3,1]);
  if(tm.some(x=>x<0||x>1||!Number.isFinite(x)))errors.push('Tone mapping range test failed');

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}
window.runRenderGroup5SelfTests=runRenderGroup5SelfTests;

function validateRenderGroup5(){

  if(typeof BasicDirectRenderer.render!=='function')errors.push('BasicDirectRenderer missing');
  if(typeof SurfaceInterpolator.normal!=='function')errors.push('SurfaceInterpolator missing');
  const tests=runRenderGroup5SelfTests();if(!tests.ok)errors.push(...tests.erro
--- occurrence 11 at 371717 ---

    this.mean[index]+=delta/s;
    this.m2[index]+=delta*(lum-this.mean[index]);
  }
  variance(index){
    const s=this.samples[index];
    return s>1?Math.max(0,this.m2[index]/(s-1)):Infinity;
  }
  noise(index){
    const mean=Math.max(1e-6,Math.abs(this.mean[index]));
    const s=Math.max(1,this.samples[index]);
    return Math.sqrt(this.variance(index)/s)/mean;
  }
  shouldSample(index,settings){
    if(!settings.adaptive)return this.samples[index]<settings.maxSamples;
    if(this.samples[index]<settings.minSamples)return true;
    if(this.samples[index]>=settings.maxSamples)return false;
    return this.noise(index)>settings.noiseThreshold;
  }
  markConverged(index){
    if(this.converged[index])return;
    this.converged[index]=1;this.active=Math.max(0,this.active-1);
  }
  resolveRGBA(settings,primaryMask){
    const rgba=new Uint8ClampedArray(this.width*this.height*4);
    for(let i=0;i<this.width*this.height;i++){
      const s=Math.max(1,this.samples[i]),o=i*3,p=i*4;
      const linear=[this.sum[o]/s,this.sum[o+1]/s,this.sum[o+2]/s];
      const mapped=RenderColor.linearToSrgb(RenderColor.aces(linear));
      rgba[p]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[0])*255);
      rgba[p+1]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[1])*255);
      rgba[p+2]=Math.round(RenderColor.clamp01(mapped[2])*255);
      rgba[p+3]=(settings.transparent&&!primaryMask[i])?0:255;
    }
    return rgba;
  }
  averageSamples(){
    let total=0;for(let i=0;i<this.samples.length;i++)total+=this.samples[i];
    return total/Math.max(1,this.samples.length);
  }
  activeIndices(settings){
    const out=new Uint32Array(this.active||this.samples.length);
    let n=0;
    for(let i=0;i<this.samples.length;i++){
      if(this.shouldSample(i,settings))out[n++]=i;
      else this.markConverged(i);
    }
    return out.subarray(0,n);
  }
  compactActive(settings){
    return this.activeIndices(settings);
  }

  averageNoise(){
    let sum=0,count=0;
    for(let i=0;i<this.samples.length;i++){
      if(this.samples[i]>1&&Number.isFinite(this.noise(i))){sum+=this.noise(i);count++;}
    }
    return count?sum/count:null;
  }
}

var PathGIRenderer=Object.freeze({
  async render(job,acceleration,onProgress=()=>{}){
    const rr=RenderRegion14.resolve(job,640,360);
    const width=rr.width,height=rr.height;
    const compiled=RenderPart5Core.compile(job);
    const settings=job.settings;
    job.lightCache=LightCacheEstimator.enabled(job)?new RenderLightCache(job,acceleration):null;

    job.pathGuide=RenderCoreH.createPathGuide(job,acceleration);
    job.progress.gi=settings.secondaryGI==='Light Cache'?'Brute Force + Light Cache':settings.secondaryGI;
    const accumulator=new ProgressiveAccumulator(width,height);
    const aovs=new RenderAOVSet(width,height);

    const rowChunk16=RenderCoreH.yieldRows(job);

    let completedPasses=0;

    for(let pass=0;pass<passLimit;pass++){
      if(job.cancelled)throw Object.assign(new Error('Render job cancelled'),{cancelled:true});

      let sampledThisPass=0;

        const a=new Uint32Array(width*height);for(let i=0;i<a.length;i++)a[i]=i;return a;
      })();
      if(activePixels16.length===0)break;

      const workQueue16=RenderWorkQueue16.fromIndices(activePixels16,Math.max(32,RenderCoreH.tileSize(job)*4));
      await workQueue16.run(async batch=>{
        for(const i of batch){
          if(job.cancelled||job.controlToken16?.cancelled?.())throw Object.assign(new Error('Render job cancelled'),{cancelled:true});
          const x=i%width,y=(i/width)|0;
          const seed=((i+1)^Math.imul(pass+1,0x9E3779B1))>>>0;

            rr.fullW,rr.fullH
          );
          perf16.addRay(RenderRayType.CAMERA);
          const sample=RenderPart5Core.trace(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,seed);
          perf16.samples++;
          primaryMask[i]|=sample.primaryHit?1:0;
          accumulator.add(i,sample.radiance);
          if(pass===0){
            const ph=sample.primaryHitRecord;
            if(ph?.hit)RenderAOVUtil.writePrimary(aovs,i,RenderAOVUtil.primary(job,acceleration,compiled,ray,ph,seed));
            else RenderAOVUtil.writePrimary(aovs,i,null);
          }
          sampledThisPass++;
        }
        perf16.yields++;
        await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,0));
      });
      completedPasses=pass+1;
      const rgba=accumulator.resolveRGBA(settings,primaryMask);
      job.previewFrame={
        width,height,rgba,
        metadata:Object.freeze({
          renderer:'Group 9 Progressive Path GI',
          samplesPerPixel:accumulator.averageSamples(),
          currentPass:completedPasses,
          maxSamples,
          adaptive:!!settings.adaptive,
          noise:accumulator.averageNoise(),
          activePixels:accumulator.active
        })
      };
      job.progress.currentSamples=Math.round(accumulator.averageSamples());
      job.progress.maximumSamples=maxSamples;
      job.progress.noise=accumulator.averageNoise();

      aovs.set3('Beauty',i,beauty);
      aovs.set1('Noise',i,Number.isFinite(accumulator.noise(i))?accumulator.noise(i):0);
      const d=aovs.get('Direct Lighting'),e=aovs.get('Emission');
      const direct=d?[d[o],d[o+1],d[o+2]]:[0,0,0];
      const emission=e?[e[o],e[o+1],e[o+2]]:[0,0,0];
      aovs.set3('Global Illumination',i,[
        Math.max(0,beauty[0]-direct[0]-emission[0]),
        Math.max(0,beauty[1]-direct[1]-emission[1]),
        Math.max(0,beauty[2]-direct[2]-emission[2])
      ]);
    }
    RenderCoreH.markStage(job,'render',renderStart16);
    if(LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance===perf16){LiteTraceExecutionContext.activeJob=null;LiteTraceExecutionContext.performance=null;}
    job.renderChannels=aovs;
    return {
      width,height,rgba,
      channels:aovs,
      metadata:Object.freeze({
        renderer:'Group 9 Progressive Path GI',
        samplesPerPixel:accumulator.averageSamples(),
        passes:completedPasses,
        maxBounces:PathIntegrator.maxDepth(job),
        estimator:'Path GI + direct NEE + power-heuristic MIS',
        mis:true,
        adaptive:!!settings.adaptive,
        progressive:!!settings.progressive,
        russianRoulette:true,
        importanceSampling:'GGX + cosine mixture',
        noise:accumulator.averageNoise(),
        transmission:true,
        secondaryGI:settings.secondaryGI,
        lightCache:job.lightCache?.stats?.()||null,
        emissiveTriangleLights:compiled.emissiveTriangles?.triangles?.length||0,
        diagnostics:RenderPart5Core.diagnostics(job,compiled,acceleration),
        performance:RenderCoreH.performance(job)
      })
    };
  }

const RenderGroup8Contract=Object.freeze({
  owns:Object.freeze([
    'multi-bounce path integration',
    'path throughput',
    'secondary-ray spawning',
    'cosine hemisphere BSDF sampling baseline',
    'environment contribution on path miss',
    'path-GI render mode'
  ]),
  consumes:Object.freeze([
    'Core B ray/BVH tracing',
    'Core C physical materials and lights',
    'Group 7 shadow visibility and direct-light sampling'
  ]),
  doesNotOwn:Object.freeze([
    'progressive accumulation',
    'adaptive sampling',
    'MIS',
    'Light Cache',
    'denoising',
    'path guiding'
  ])
});

function runRenderGroup8SelfTests(){
  const errors=[];

  {
    const n=[0,0,1];
    const d=PathSampler.cosineHemisphere(n,123,0);
    if(!Number.isFinite(d[0]+d[1]+d[2])||RayMath.dot(n,d)<-1e-6){
      errors.push('Cosine hemisphere sample failed');
    }
  }

  {
    const mat=new RenderSurfaceMaterial({
      id:'p8',name:'p8',shading:'physical',baseColor:[.8,.8,.8],
      roughness:.6,metalness:0,opacity:1,emissive:[0,0,0],emissiveIntensity:0
    });
    const smp=PathBSDFSampler.sample(mat,[0,0,1],[0,0,1],456,1);
    if(!smp||!Number.isFinite(smp.pdf)||smp.pdf<=0||smp.weight.some(x=>!Number.isFinite(x)||x<0)){
      errors.push('Path BSDF sample failed');
    }
  }

  {
    const fakeJob={settings:{maxBounces:99}};
    if(PathIntegrator.maxDepth(fakeJob)!==16)errors.push('Path depth clamp failed');
  }

  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}
window.runRenderGroup8SelfTests=runRenderGroup8SelfTests;

function validateRenderGroup8(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof PathSampler.cosineHemisphere!=='function')errors.push('PathSampler missing');
  if(typeof PathBSDFSampler.sample!=='function')errors.push('PathBSDFSampler missing');
  if(typeof PathIntegrator.trace!=='function')errors.push('PathIntegrator missing');
  if(typeof PathGIRenderer.render!=='function')errors.push('PathGIRenderer missing');
  const tests=runRenderGroup8SelfTests();if(!tests.ok)errors.push(...tests.errors);
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

const orbit=new THREE.OrbitControls(camera,renderer.domElement);

// Use a custom adaptive wheel dolly instead of OrbitControls' percentage-only zoom.
// The stock dolly becomes progressively tiny as camera-target distance approaches zero.
orbit.enableZoom=false;
orbit.update();

const VIEW_ZOOM={
  fraction:0.115,      // normal zoom remains proportional at medium/long distance
  minStep:3.0,        // cm: prevents wheel zoom from becoming almost stationary
  closeDistance:18.0, // cm: below this, move target with camera so dolly can continue
  maxFraction:0.22
};

function adaptiveViewportWheelZoom(e){
  if(e.ctrlKey)return; // leave browser/system Ctrl+wheel behavior alone
  e.preventDefault();
  e.stopPropagation();

  const delta=Math.sign(e.deltaY);
  if(!delta)return;

  const toTarget=new THREE.Vector3().subVectors(orbit.target,camera.position);
  let distance=toTarget.length();
  if(distance<1e-6){
    camera.getWorldDirection(toTarget);
    distance=VIEW_ZOOM.closeDistance;
  }else{
    toTarget.multiplyScalar(1/distance);
  }

  // Normalize trackpads/mice without allowing a single event to jump excessively.
  const wheelUnits=Math.min(3,Math.max(1,Math.abs(e.deltaY)/100));
  const proportional=Math.min(distance*VIEW_ZOOM.maxFraction,
                              Math.max(distance*VIEW_ZOOM.fraction,VIEW_ZOOM.minStep));
  let step=proportional*wheelUnits;

  if(delta<0){ // zoom in
    if(distance>VIEW_ZOOM.closeDistance){
      // Approach the orbit target normally, but never collapse onto it.
      step=Math.min(step,Math.max(VIEW_ZOOM.minStep,distance-VIEW_ZOOM.closeDistance));
      camera.position.addScaledVector(toTarget,step);
    }else{
      // Near the target, translate camera and target together. This prevents
      // the familiar "zoom barely moves near the object" stall.
      camera.position.addScaledVector(toTarget,step);
      orbit.target.addScaledVector(toTarget,step);
    }
  }else{ // zoom out
    camera.position.addScaledVector(toTarget,-step);
  }

  orbit.update();
}

renderer.domElement.addEventListener('wheel',adaptiveViewportWheelZoom,{passive:false,capture:true});

transform.setMode('translate');
transform.setSpace('local');
transform.setSize(.9);
transform.showX=transform.showY=transform.showZ=true;
scene.add(transform);


/* Classic Minimal transform-widget visual theme. Geometry and hit testing remain native. */
const ClassicMinimalTransformTheme=Object.freeze({
  x:0xe4574f,y:0x4ecb64,z:0x3f7cff,neutral:0xd7d7d7,screen:0xbfc3c7,
  planeOpacity:.18,handleOpacity:.96
});
function classicMinimalColorForHandle(name){
  if(name==='X')return ClassicMinimalTransformTheme.x;
  if(name==='Y')return ClassicMinimalTransformTheme.y;
  if(name==='Z')return ClassicMinimalTransformTheme.z;
  if(name==='XY')return ClassicMinimalTransformTheme.z;
  if(name==='YZ')return ClassicMinimalTransformTheme.x;
  if(name==='XZ')return ClassicMinimalTransformTheme.y;
  if(name==='XYZ'||name==='XYZE')return ClassicMinimalTransformTheme.neutral;
  if(name==='E')return ClassicMinimalTransformTheme.screen;
  return null;
}
function applyClassicMinimalTransformTheme(){
  transform.traverse(ch=>{
    const name=ch.name||'';
    const color=classicMinimalColorForHandle(name);
    if(color==null||!ch.material)return;
    const mats=Array.isArray(ch.material)?ch.material:[ch.material];
    for(const m of mats){
      if(!m||!m.color||m.opacity===0)continue;
      m.color.setHex(color);
      if(m._color?.setHex)m._color.setHex(color);
      const isPlane=name.length===2 && name!=='E';
      const op=isPlane?ClassicMinimalTransformTheme.planeOpacity:ClassicMinimalTransformTheme.handleOpacity;
      if(m.opacity>0){m.transparent=isPlane||m.transparent;m.opacity=Math.min(m.opacity,op);}
      if('_opacity' in m && m._opacity>0)m._opacity=Math.min(m._opacity,op);
      m.depthWrite=false;
      m.needsUpdate=true;
    }
  });
  uniformScaleHandle?.material?.color?.setHex(ClassicMinimalTransformTheme.neutral);
  if(uniformScaleHandle?.material){uniformScaleHandle.material.opacity=.94;uniformScaleHandle.material.needsUpdate=true;}
}

  if(mode==='rotate')return 'var(--cursor-rotate)';
  if(mode==='scale'){
    if(axis==

      }));

    }

    return new PreparedGeometrySet(items);
  }
};


/* ============================================================
   3DLite v3.82.8 — LiteTrace Renderer v1.1 RC1 ASI Fix — CORE B
   CLEAN GROUP 3 + GROUP 4 CONSOLIDATION

class RenderExecutionGeometry{
  constructor(renderScene){
    this.accelerationPackets=RenderGeometryCompiler.compileScene(renderScene);
    this.workerPackets=this.accelerationPackets.map(p=>new RenderGeometryPacket({
      objectId:p.objectId,name:p.name,
      positions:new Float32Array(p.positions),
      normals:new Float32Array(p.normals),
      uvs:new Float32Array(p.uvs),
      indices:new Uint32Array(p.indices),
      materialIndices:new Uint16Array(p.materialIndices),
      bounds:{min:[...p.bounds.min],max:[...p.bounds.max]}
    }));
    Object.freeze(this.accelerationPackets);
    Object.freeze(this.workerPackets);
  }
}

var RenderCoreB=Object.freeze({
  version:'3.79.16-core-b-part2',

  createExecutionGeometry(renderScene){
    return new RenderExecutionGeometry(renderScene);
  },

  buildAccelerationFromPackets(packets){
    const store=new RenderTriangleStore();
    packets.forEach((p,i)=>store.addPacket(p,i));
    store.finalize();
    return new RenderAccelerationSet(store,new RenderBVH(store,4));
  },

  async prepareWorkerGeometry(job,pool,executionGeometry,onProgress=()=>{}){
    const items=[],packets=executionGeometry.workerPackets,total=packets.length;
    for(let i=0;i<packets.length;i++){
      if(job.cancelled)throw Object.assign(new Error('Render job cancelled'),{cancelled:true});
      while(job.paused&&!job.cancelled)await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,25));

      const packet=packets[i];
      let result;
      try{
        result=await pool.run(
          {type:'prepareGeometry',jobId:job.id,packet},
          RenderGeometryCompiler.transferables(packet)
        );
      }catch(err){
        if(err?.workerUnavailable){job.progress.workerFallback=true;result=RenderGeometryPreparation.mainThread(job,packet);}
        else throw err;
      }

      items.push(Object.freeze({
        objectId:result.objectId,
        vertexCount:result.vertexCount,
        triangleCount:result.triangleCount,
        bounds:Object.freeze(Array.from(result.bounds||[])),
        triangleIndices:result.triangleIndices
      }));
      onProgress((i+1)/Math.max(1,total),items[items.length-1]);
    }
    return new PreparedGeometrySet(items);
  },

  validate(){
    const errors=[];
    const g3=validateRenderGroup3();if(!g3.ok)errors.push(...g3.errors);
    const g4=validateRenderGroup4();if(!g4.ok)errors.push(...g4.errors);
    if(typeof this.createExecutionGeometry!=='function')errors.push('Core B execution geometry missing');
    if(typeof this.buildAccelerationFromPackets!=='function')errors.push('Core B acceleration builder missing');
    return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
  }
});
window.RenderCoreB=RenderCoreB;

const RenderGroup3Contract=Object.freeze({
  owns:Object.freeze([
    'worker pool',
    'render execution queue',
    'worker-safe geometry packets',
    'render geometry preparation',
    'prepared triangle-index payloads'
  ]),
  consumes:Object.freeze([
    'RenderCoreA render jobs',
    'immutable RenderSceneSnapshot'
  ]),
  doesNotOwn:Object.freeze([
    'editor objects',
    'editor geometry buffers',
    'MNMesh',
    'viewport renderer',
    'selection',
    'transforms',
    'editor undo'
  ])
});

function validateRenderGroup3(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof RenderWorkerPool!=='function')errors.push('RenderWorkerPool missing');
  if(typeof RenderGeometryCompiler.compileScene!=='function')errors.push('RenderGeometryCompiler missing');
  if(typeof RenderGeometryValidation.validatePacket!=='function')errors.push('RenderGeometryValidation missing');
  if(typeof RenderGeometryPreparation.prepare!=='function')errors.push('RenderGeometryPreparation missing');
  if(typeof PreparedGeometrySet!=='function')errors.push('PreparedGeometrySet missing');
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

Object.assign(window,{
  RenderGeometryPacket,
  RenderGeometryCompiler,RenderGeometryValidation,
  RenderWorkerPool,
  PreparedGeometrySet,
  RenderGeometryPreparation,
  RenderGroup3Contract
});

/* ============================================================
   3DLite v3.82.8 — LiteTrace Renderer v1.1 RC1 ASI Fix — GROUP 4
   Rays + Triangle Intersections + BVH + Camera
   ============================================================ */
const RayMath=Object.freeze({
  EPSILON:1e-6,
  add(a,b){return [a[0]+b[0],a[1]+b[1],a[2]+b[2]]},
  sub(a,b){return [a[0]-b[0],a[1]-b[1],a[2]-b[2]]},
  mul(a,s){return [a[0]*s,a[1]*s,a[2]*s]},
  dot(a,b){return a[0]*b[0]+a[1]*b[1]+a[2]*b[2]},
  cross(a,b){return [a[1]*b[2]-a[2]*b[1],a[2]*b[0]-a[0]*b[2],a[0]*b[1]-a[1]*b[0]]},
  length(a){return Math.hypot(a[0],a[1],a[2])},
  normalize(a){const l=Math.hypot(a[0],a[1],a[2])||1;return [a[0]/l,a[1]/l,a[2]/l]},
  min3(a,b){return [Math.min(a[0],b[0]),Math.min(a[1],b[1]),Math.min(a[2],b[2])]},
  max3(a,b){return [Math.max(a[0],b[0]),Math.max(a[1],b[1]),Math.max(a[2],b[2])]}
});

const RenderRayType=Object.freeze({CAMERA:'camera',SHADOW:'shadow',DIFFUSE:'diffuse',GLOSSY:'glossy',TRANSMISSION:'transmission'});
class RenderRay{
  constructor(origin,direction,tMin=1e-5,tMax=Infinity,type=RenderRayType.CAMERA,depth=0){
    this.origin=origin;this.direction=RayMath.normalize(direction);
    this.invDirection=[
      Math.abs(this.direction[0])<RayMath.EPSILON?Infinity:1/this.direction[0],
      Math.abs(this.direction[1])<RayMath.EPSILON?Infinity:1/this.direction[1],
      Math.abs(this.direction[2])<RayMath.EPSILON?Infinity:1/this.direction[2]
    ];
    this.tMin=tMin;this.tMax=tMax;this.type=type;this.depth=depth;
  }
  at(t){return RayMath.add(this.origin,RayMath.mul(this.direction,t))}
}
const RenderRayPolicy=Object.freeze({
  epsilonForPoint(p){const scale=Math.max(1,Math.abs(p[0]),Math.abs(p[1]),Math.abs(p[2]));return Math.min(1e-2,Math.max(1e-6,scale*1e-7));}
});
Object.assign(window,{RenderRayType,RenderRayPolicy});

class RenderHit{
  constructor(){
    this.hit=false;this.t=I
--- occurrence 5 at 273150 ---
ull;this.acceleration=null;},
  revision(scene){
    const m=scene?.metadata||{};
    return [m.sceneRevision??'s?',m.geometryRevision??'g?',m.materialRevision??'m?',m.lightRevision??'l?',scene?.objects?.length||0,
      ...(scene?.objects||[]).map(o=>`${o.id}:${o.visible!==false?1:0}:${o.mesh?.positions?.length||0}:${o.mesh?.indices?.length||0}`)
    ].join('|');
  },
  get(scene){
    const rev=this.revision(scene);
    if(this.acceleration&&this.geometryRevision===rev){this.hits++;return this.acceleration;}
    this.misses++;return null;
  },
  put(scene,acceleration){
    this.geometryRevision=this.revision(scene);
    this.acceleration=acceleration;
    return acceleration;
  },
  stats(){return Object.freeze({geometryRevision:this.geometryRevision,hits:this.hits,misses:this.misses,reused:!!this.acceleration});}
};

/*
  BLAS/TLAS boundary:
  - object mesh geometry revisions feed BLAS-equivalent cached acceleration data;
  - scene/object transform revisions are intentionally kept outside this cache key
    so a future TLAS can update instances without rebuilding object geometry.
  Current browser renderer still uses the proven binary RenderAccelerationSet.
*/
const RenderAccelerationHierarchy16=Object.freeze({
  mode:'geometry-revision acceleration cache; TLAS/BLAS boundary reserved',
  cache:RenderAccelerationCache16,
  build(scene,builder){
    const cached=RenderAccelerationCache16.get(scene);
    if(cached)return cached;
    return RenderAccelerationCache16.put(scene,builder());
  }
});
window.RenderAccelerationCache16=RenderAccelerationCache16;
window.RenderAccelerationHierarchy16=RenderAccelerationHierarchy16;

const RenderAccelerationBuilder=Object.freeze({
  build(renderScene){
    return RenderAccelerationHierarchy16.build(renderScene,()=>{
      const packets=RenderGeometryCompiler.compileScene(renderScene);
      const store=new RenderTriangleStore();
      packets.forEach((p,i)=>store.addPacket(p,i));
      store.finalize();
      return new RenderAccelerationSet(store,new RenderBVH(store,4));
    });
  }
});

const DiagnosticRenderer=Object.freeze({
  async render(job,acceleration,onProgress=()=>{}){
    const width=Math.min(640,Math.max(1,job.progress.resolution[0]));
    const height=Math.min(360,Math.max(1,job.progress.resolution[1]));
    const rgba=new Uint8ClampedArray(width*height*4);
    const cameraSnapshot=job.renderScene.camera;
    const rowChunk=8;
    for(let y=0;y<height;y++){
      if(job.cancelled||job.controlToken16?.cancelled?.())throw Object.assign(new Error('Render job cancelled'),{cancelled:true});
      while((job.paused||job.controlToken16?.paused?.())&&!job.cancelled)await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,20));
      for(let x=0;x<width;x++){
        const ray=RenderCameraRayGenerator.ray(cameraSnapshot,x,y,width,height);
        perf16.addRay(RenderRayType.CAMERA);
        perf16.addRay(RenderRayType.CAMERA);
        const hit=acceleration.trace(ray);
        const o=(y*width+x)*4;
        if(hit.hit){
          const n=hit.geometricNormal;
          rgba[o]=Math.round((n[0]*.5+.5)*255);
          rgba[o+1]=Math.round((n[1]*.5+.5)*255);
          rgba[o+2]=Math.round((n[2]*.5+.5)*255);
          rgba[o+3]=255;
        }else{
          rgba[o]=24;rgba[o+1]=24;rgba[o+2]=24;rgba[o+3]=255;
        }
      }
      if(y%rowChunk===0)onProgress((y+1)/height);
      if(y%rowChunk===0)await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,0));
    }
    return {width,height,rgba};
  }
});

/*
  BLAS/TLAS boundary:
  - object mesh geometry revisions feed BLAS-equivalent cached acceleration data;
  - scene/object transform revisions are intentionally kept outside this cache key
    so a future TLAS can update instances without rebuilding object geometry.
  Current browser renderer still uses the proven binary RenderAccelerationSet.
*/
const RenderAccelerationHierarchy16=Object.freeze({
  mode:'geometry-revision acceleration cache; TLAS/BLAS boundary reserved',
  cache:RenderAccelerationCache16,
  build(scene,builder){
    const cached=RenderAccelerationCache16.get(scene);
    if(cached)return cached;
    return RenderAccelerationCache16.put(scene,builder());
  }
});
window.RenderAccelerationCache16=RenderAccelerationCache16;
window.RenderAccelerationHierarchy16=RenderAccelerationHierarchy16;

const RenderAccelerationBuilder=Object.freeze({
  build(renderScene){
    return RenderAccelerationHierarchy16.build(renderScene,()=>{
      const packets=RenderGeometryCompiler.compileScene(renderScene);
      const store=new RenderTriangleStore();
      packets.forEach((p,i)=>store.addPacket(p,i));
      store.finalize();
      return new RenderAccelerationSet(store,new RenderBVH(store,4));
    });
  }
});

const DiagnosticRenderer=Object.freeze({
  async render(job,acceleration,onProgress=()=>{}){
    const width=Math.min(640,Math.max(1,job.progress.resolution[0]));
    const height=Math.min(360,Math.max(1,job.progress.resolution[1]));
    const rgba=new Uint8ClampedArray(width*height*4);
    const cameraSnapshot=job.renderScene.camera;
    const rowChunk=8;
    for(let y=0;y<height;y++){
      if(job.cancelled||job.controlToken16?.cancelled?.())throw Object.assign(new Error('Render job cancelled'),{cancelled:true});
      while((job.paused||job.controlToken16?.paused?.())&&!job.cancelled)await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,20));
      for(let x=0;x<width;x++){
        const ray=RenderCameraRayGenerator.ray(cameraSnapshot,x,y,width,height);
        perf16.addRay(RenderRayType.CAMERA);
        perf16.addRay(RenderRayType.CAMERA);
        const hit=acceleration.trace(ray);
        const o=(y*width+x)*4;
        if(hit.hit){
          const n=hit.geometricNormal;
          rgba[o]=Math.round((n[0]*.5+.5)*255);
          rgba[o+1]=Math.round((n[1]*.5+.5)*255);
          rgba[o+2]=Math.round((n[2]*.5+.5)*255);
          rgba[o+3]=255;
        }else{
          rgba[o]=24;rgba[o+1]=24;rgba[o+2]=24;rgba[o+3]=255;
        }
      }
      if(y%rowChunk===0)onProgress((y+1)/height);
      if(y%rowChunk===0)await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,0));
    }
    return {width,height,rgba};
  }
});

const RenderPerformancePolicy16=Object.freeze({
  tileSize(job){
    const raw=String(job?.settings?.tileSize||'Auto');
    if(raw==='Auto'){
      const q=String(job?.settings?.quality||'Preview');
      const w=Math.max(1,job?.progress?.resolution?.[0]||640);
      const h=Math.max(1,job?.progress?.resolution?.[1]||360);
      const pixels=w*h;
      const threads=Math.max(1,Number(job?.progress?.threads)||Math.max(1,(navigator.hardwareConcurrency||2)-1));
      let size=q==='Draft'?32:q==='Ultra'?8:16;
      if(pixels>1920*1080&&threads>=8)size=Math.max(size,32);
      if(pixels<640*480)size=Math.min(size,16);
      if(threads<=2)size=Math.max(size,32);
      return size;
    }
    const n=Number(raw);
    return [8,16,32,64].includes(n)?n:16;
  },
  yieldRows(job){
    // Tile Size controls cooperative row batches only.
    // This deliberately does not replace the proven scanline traversal.
    return Math.max(1,Math.min(16,Math.round(this.tileSize(job)/4)));
  }
});



const LiteTraceScratch=Object.freeze({
  v3a:new Float64Array(3),v3b:new Float64Array(3),v3c:new Float64Array(3),rgb:new Float64Array(3)
});
window.LiteTraceScratch=LiteTraceScratch;

var RenderProfiler16=Object.freeze({
  now(){return performance.now();},
  begin(){return performance.now();},
  elapsed(start){return Math.max(0,performance.now()-start);},
  mark(counter,name,start){
    const ms=this.elapsed(start);
    if(counter?.stageMs&&name in counter.stageMs)counter.stageMs[name]+=ms;
    return ms;
  }
});
window.RenderProfiler16=RenderProfiler16;

var RenderControlToken16 = class RenderControlToken16{
  constructor(){
    this.shared=typeof SharedArrayBuffer!=='undefined'&&typeof Atomics!=='undefined';
    this.buffer=this.shared?new SharedArrayBuffer(Int32Array.BYTES_PER_ELEMENT*3):new ArrayBuffer(Int32Array.BYTES_PER_ELEMENT*3);
    this.flags=new Int32Array(this.buffer); // [cancel,pause,generation]
    this.flags[2]=1;
  }
  cancel(){this.shared?Atomics.store(this.flags,0,1):(this.flags[0]=1);}
  reset(){this.shared?(Atomics.store(this.flags,0,0),Atomics.store(this.flags,1,0),Atomics.add(this.flags,2,1)):(this.flags[0]=0,this.flags[1]=0,this.flags[2]++);}
  pause(){this.shared?Atomics.store(this.flags,1,1):(this.flags[1]=1);}
  resume(){this.shared?Atomics.store(this.flags,1,0):(this.flags[1]=0);}
  cancelled(){return (this.shared?Atomics.load(this.flags,0):this.flags[0])!==0;}
  paused(){return (this.shared?Atomics.load(this.flags,1):this.flags[1])!==0;}
  generation(){return this.shared?Atomics.load(this.flags,2):this.flags[2];}
}
window.RenderControlToken16=RenderControlToken16;

var RenderPerformanceCounters16 = class RenderPerformanceCounters16{
  constructor(){
    this.cameraRays=0;
    this.pathRays=0;
    this.shadowRays=0;
    this.probeRays=0;
    this.samples=0;
    this.yields=0;
    this.stageMs={snapshot:0,geometry:0,worker:0,bvh:0,shading:0,lightCache:0,render:0,denoise:0,toneMap:0,post:0};
    this.startedAt=RenderProfiler16.now();
  }
  addRay(type){
    if(type===RenderRayType.CAMERA)this.cameraRays++;
    else if(type===RenderRayType.SHADOW)this.shadowRays++;
    else this.pathRays++;
  }
  addProbe(){this.probeRays++;}
  markStage(name,start){
    return RenderProfiler16.mark(this,name,start);
  }
  snapshot(){
    const elapsed=Math.max(.001,performance.now()-this.startedAt);
    const totalRays=this.cameraRays+this.pathRays;
    return Object.freeze({
      cameraRays:this.cameraRays,
      pathRays:this.pathRays,
      shadowRays:this.shadowRays,
      probeRays:this.probeRays,
      totalRays:totalRays+this.shadowRays+this.probeRays,
      samples:this.samples,
      yields:this.yields,
      raysPerSecond:Math.round(totalRays/(elapsed/1000)),
      stageMs:Object.freeze({...this.stageMs})
    });
  }
}

const RenderPerformanceRuntime16=Object.freeze({
  attach(job){
    if(!job.performanceState)job.performanceState=new RenderPerformanceCounters16();
    return job.performanceState;
  },
  markStage(job,name,start){
    this.attach(job).markStage(name,start);
  },
  snapshot(job){
    return job?.performanceState?.snapshot?.()||null;
  }
});

  generation(){return this.shared?Atomics.load(this.flags,2):this.flags[2];}
}

  }

    if(type===RenderRayType.CAMERA)this.cameraRays++;

const RenderPerformancePolicy16=Object.freeze({
  tileSize(job){
    const raw=String(job?.settings?.tileSize||'Auto');
    if(raw==='Auto'){
      const q=String(job?.settings?.quality||'Preview');
      const w=Math.max(1,job?.progress?.resolution?.[0]||640);
      const h=Math.max(1,job?.progress?.resolution?.[1]||360);
      const pixels=w*h;
      const threads=Math.max(1,Number(job?.progress?.threads)||Math.max(1,(navigator.hardwareConcurrency||2)-1));
      let size=q==='Draft'?32:q==='Ultra'?8:16;
      if(pixels>1920*1080&&threads>=8)size=Math.max(size,32);
      if(pixels<640*480)size=Math.min(size,16);
      if(threads<=2)size=Math.max(size,32);
      return size;
    }
    const n=Number(raw);
    return [8,16,32,64].includes(n)?n:16;
  },
  yieldRows(job){
    // Tile Size controls cooperative row batches only.
    // This deliberately does not replace the proven scanline traversal.
    return Math.max(1,Math.min(16,Math.round(this.tileSize(job)/4)));
  }
});



const LiteTraceScratch=Object.freeze({
  v3a:new Float64Array(3),v3b:new Float64Array(3),v3c:new Float64Array(3),rgb:new Float64Array(3)
});
window.LiteTraceScratch=LiteTraceScratch;

var RenderProfiler16=Object.freeze({
  now(){return performance.now();},
  begin(){return performance.now();},
  elapsed(start){return Math.max(0,performance.now()-start);},
  mark(counter,name,start){
    const ms=this.elapsed(start);
    if(counter?.stageMs&&name in counter.stageMs)counter.stageMs[name]+=ms;
    return ms;
  }
});
window.RenderProfiler16=RenderProfiler16;

var RenderControlToken16 = class RenderControlToken16{
  constructor(){
    this.shared=typeof SharedArrayBuffer!=='undefined'&&typeof Atomics!=='undefined';
    this.buffer=this.shared?new SharedArrayBuffer(Int32Array.BYTES_PER_ELEMENT*3):new ArrayBuffer(Int32Array.BYTES_PER_ELEMENT*3);
    this.flags=new Int32Array(this.buffer); // [cancel,pause,generation]
    this.flags[2]=1;
  }
  cancel(){this.shared?Atomics.store(this.flags,0,1):(this.flags[0]=1);}
  reset(){this.shared?(Atomics.store(this.flags,0,0),Atomics.store(this.flags,1,0),Atomics.add(this.flags,2,1)):(this.flags[0]=0,this.flags[1]=0,this.flags[2]++);}
  pause(){this.shared?Atomics.store(this.flags,1,1):(this.flags[1]=1);}
  resume(){this.shared?Atomics.store(this.flags,1,0):(this.flags[1]=0);}
  cancelled(){return (this.shared?Atomics.load(this.flags,0):this.flags[0])!==0;}
  paused(){return (this.shared?Atomics.load(this.flags,1):this.flags[1])!==0;}
  generation(){return this.shared?Atomics.load(this.flags,2):this.flags[2];}
}
window.RenderControlToken16=RenderControlToken16;

const RenderGroup16Contract=Object.freeze({
  owns:Object.freeze([
    'tile-size cooperative row-batch policy',
    'camera/path ray counters',
    'sample counter',
    'cooperative yield counter',
    'render-stage timing',
    'rays-per-second diagnostics'
  ]),

    'Group 15 Light Tree and Path Guiding',
    'Group 14 region rendering',
    'editor startup order',
    'viewport ownership'
  ]),

    'editor startup','viewport','selection','modeling',

function runRenderGroup16SelfTests(){
  const errors=[],expect=(c,m)=>{if(!c)errors.push(m);};
  try{
    expect(RenderPerformancePolicy16.tileSize({settings:{tileSize:'8'}})===8,'Tile size 8 failed');
    expect(RenderPerformancePolicy16.tileSize({settings:{tileSize:'64'}})===64,'Tile size 64 failed');
    expect(RenderPerformancePolicy16.tileSize({settings:{tileSize:'Auto',quality:'Draft'}})===32,'Auto Draft tile failed');
    expect(RenderPerformancePolicy16.yieldRows({settings:{tileSize:'16'}})===4,'Yield-row policy failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push('Performance policy: '+String(e?.message||e));}
  try{
    const c=new RenderPerformanceCounters16();
    c.addRay(RenderRayType.CAMERA);
    c.addRay(RenderRayType.DIFFUSE);
    c.samples=2;
    const snap=c.snapshot();
    expect(snap.totalRays===2&&snap.cameraRays===1&&snap.pathRays===1,'Ray counters failed');
    expect(snap.samples===2&&Number.isFinite(snap.raysPerSecond),'Sample/RPS counters failed');
  }catch(e){errors.push('Performance counters: '+String(e?.message||e));}
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

function validateRenderGroup16(){
  const errors=[];
  if(typeof RenderPerformancePolicy16?.tileSize!=='function')errors.push('Performance policy missing');
  if(typeof RenderPerformanceCounters16!=='function')errors.push('Performance counters missing');
  if(typeof RenderPerformanceRuntime16?.attach!=='function')errors.push('Performance runtime missing');
  const t=runRenderGroup16SelfTests();
  if(!t.ok)errors.push(...t.errors);
  return {ok:errors.length===0,errors};
}

const RenderGroup16Core=Object.freeze({
  version:'3.79.31-group16-safe-rewrite-gen3',
  validate:validateRenderGroup16,
  policy:RenderPerformancePolicy16,
  runtime:RenderPerformanceRuntime16
});

Object.assign(window,{
  RenderPerformancePolicy16,RenderPerformanceCounters16,RenderPerformanceRuntime16,
  RenderGroup16Contract,runRenderGroup16SelfTests,validateRenderGroup16,RenderGroup16Core
});


var RenderRayBatch16 = class RenderRayBatch16{
  constructor(capacity=128){
    this.capacity=Math.max(1,capacity|0);
    this.origins=new Float64Array(this.capacity*3);
    this.directions=new Float64Array(this.capacity*3);
    this.pixelIndices=new Uint32Array(this.capacity);
    this.seeds=new Uint32Array(this.capacity);
    this.count=0;
  }
  clear(){this.count=0;return this;}
  push(ray,pixelIndex,seed){
    if(this.count>=this.capacity)return false;
    const i=this.count++,o=i*3;
    this.origins[o]=ray.origin[0];this.origins[o+1]=ray.origin[1];this.origins[o+2]=ray.origin[2];
    this.directions[o]=ray.direction[0];this.directions[o+1]=ray.direction[1];this.directions[o+2]=ray.direction[2];
    this.pixelIndices[i]=pixelIndex>>>0;this.seeds[i]=seed>>>0;
    return true;
  }
  ray(i,type=RenderRayType.CAMERA){
    const o=i*3;
    return new RenderRay(
      [this.origins[o],this.origins[o+1],this.origins[o+2]],
      [this.directions[o],this.directions[o+1],this.directions[o+2]],
      1e-6,Infinity,type,0
    );
  }
  trace(acceleration,type=RenderRayType.CAMERA){
    const hits=new Array(this.count);
    for(let i=0;i<this.count;i++)hits[i]=acceleration.trace(this.ray(i,type));
    return hits;
  }
}
window.RenderRayBatch16=RenderRayBatch16;

var RenderWorkQueue16 = class RenderWorkQueue16{
  constructor(chunks){
    this.queue=chunks.slice();
    this.completed=0;
    this.claimed=0;
    this.steals=0;
  }
  static fromIndices(indices,batchSize=128){
    const chunks=[];
    const n=indices.length;
    const adaptive=Math.max(16,Math.min(512,Math.round(batchSize*(n>250000?.5:n<10000?2:1))));
    for(let i=0;i<n;i+=adaptive){
      chunks.push(typeof indices.subarray==='function'?indices.subarray(i,Math.min(n,i+adaptive)):indices.slice(i,i+adaptive));
    }
    return new RenderWorkQueue16(chunks);
  }
